# Circuit-level results

Item set: the item file is the set of accepted pairs (the accepted pairs).

Items: 227 (moral 80, norm 107, descr 40, prudential 15, etiquette 18, aesthetic 22, epistemic 24); inclusion S(perm) - S(rest) >= 0.02; subset: accepted; bootstrap 2000 item resamples.
These analyses are exploratory.

## Necessity (mean-ablation of head sets against size-matched random sets)

Fraction of the clean gap removed = 1 - mean(gap after ablation) / mean(clean gap) over the included items of a group (ratio of means). Rank = number of random sets of the same size that remove at least as much as the located set (0 means the located set beats all of them; with 20 random sets, a rank of 0 corresponds to the 95th percentile). Mode `all`: every non-BOS position ablated; `final`: the final position only (the cleaner test).

## Roles (what the heads attend to, and what they write)

Attention: weight of the final position (the readout position) on each token group, averaged over the two prompts, the included items and the heads of a set; `all heads` is the mean over every head of the model. Direct effect: perm-minus-rest difference of the direct logit attribution to the logit difference (positive words minus negative words), summed over the heads of a set; the share is its ratio to the total difference (embedding + all heads + all MLPs, which equals the metric difference exactly).

## Layers (where the difference is computed and where it travels)

Fraction of the gap recovered = mean(S after patching layer l) - mean(S rest) over mean(clean gap), p2r. `half-layer final`: first layer at which patching the residual stream at the final position recovers at least half of the gap; `half-layer edit`: last layer at which patching the residual at the edited tokens still recovers at least half. Both also as a fraction of the depth.

## Edges (which components feed the heads, through which input)

Score of an edge = first-order estimate of the change of the logit difference when that edge alone is patched from the permissive into the restrictive run (p2r), mean over the included items of a group; sign kept (positive = pushes the restrictive prompt towards the permissive answer). Sources: every head, every MLP block, the embedding. `sign consistency`: share of items whose score has the sign of the mean. The validation of the estimate against exact edge patching is printed by run_edges.py --validate.

### Strongest upstream edges into the located heads and the logits (moral items; three per receiver across its query, key and value inputs; the 10 strongest per input, for every receiver and group, are in `edges_top.csv`)

**Llama-3.1-8B, A1** (n = 78)

* L12H22: MLP11 -> v (+0.086, sign 0.83), L11H23 -> v (-0.031, sign 0.90), MLP9 -> v (+0.027, sign 0.64)
* L12H4: MLP10 -> v (+0.054, sign 0.83), MLP8 -> v (+0.038, sign 0.78), L9H26 -> v (+0.028, sign 0.95)
